Twice during the clip, Tux does something that releases a group of new coins. From the first of those moments until the end of the clip, the released coins do not behave the way coins in this game should. The coins that were already placed in the level, Tux, the animals and the scenery in the recording are correct.\n\n### Expected\nEach group of released coins should behave like a normal coin burst in this game, from the moment they spread out of their block until they come to rest and Tux goes back to collect them. Once a coin is within the basin's surrounding environment, it should drift down slowly, from the moment it enters it. Fix every way the released coins behave wrongly in the recording.\n\n## Attached visual evidence\n\n- `/app/.harbor_assets/recording.mp4` (47 seconds)\n\nYou can inspect individual moments of the video by extracting frames from it with `ffmpeg`.\n\nInspect the attached file before making changes.\n=== task.toml ===\nversion = \"1.0\"\nreward_type = \"binary\"\n\n[task]\nname = \"scale-ai/supertux__supertux_3729_mm\"\n\n[metadata]\ntask_id = \"supertux__supertux_3729_mm\"\ntitle = \"Some coins misbehave in the flooded cave\"\nrepository_name = \"SuperTux/supertux\"\nrepo_url = \"https://github.com/SuperTux/supertux\"\nissue_number = 3729\npr_number = 3771\nbase_commit = \"4b5663fc0c3ba08a8f8bd2ec8b75b5c02f82f7c7\"\nfix_commit = \"d1d16705985c881346fedd1e29ac4dbafa071604\"\ntask_type = \"temporal_interaction\"\nlanguage = \"C++\"\nframework = \"SuperTux\"\ncross_file = true\nvisual_essentiality = \"required\"\ndifficulty = \"hard\"\ndifficulty_explanation = \"\"\"\nFive independent defects plus the upstream missing feature, all in the coin-burst pipeline (src/object/coin_explode.cpp and src/object/coin.cpp), all visible only as motion in the attached recording and all affecting the same coins at once: (1) the burst's velocity table is a function-static list that is scaled in place, so the first burst of the session looks right but every later one is thrown thousands of times too hard and the coins rocket out of sight (the clip opens two blocks, the second shows it); (2) heavy coins ignore water entirely, so coins reach the bottom at full speed (the upstream issue #3729), and copying Rock's gravity modifier is not enough because coins that hit the water while falling fast must also be slowed; (3) a coin that lands keeps its horizontal speed and skates along the floor; (4) the released coins are put in a collision group that never meets the player, so Tux swims straight through them; (5) the landing bounce threshold is so low that every landing counts as a bounce, so landed coins keep hopping; (6) a heavy coin is deleted as soon as it is below the bottom edge of the camera view, through a helper called update_visibility() that reads like a rendering step, so resting coins vanish whenever Tux swims up and away and are gone when he comes back to collect them.\n\nThe text names none of these. Defects (1) and (6) only show at specific moments of the clip ((1) for about three frames on the second burst, (6) only once the coins leave the screen), and (6) also hides (4) at the end of the clip: the floor is empty when Tux returns, so the pickup failure is only visible earlier, when he sweeps the floor through the resting coins without collecting any. Each defect has its own behavioural f2p test run in the real game binary (scripted levels, no source inspection): a two-block repeat-burst room with open sky, and a tall shaft where a coin rests far below the camera, in addition to the water and burst levels.
